# B25 LLM Vision UX Review - b25-v4-pass-28

- Reviewer type: llm-vision-ux-judge
- Fresh review: true
- App commit SHA: 00ce87a
- Reviewed screen rows: 195
- Reviewed unique screenshot hashes: 184
- Final decision: fail

## Holistic Answers
- **PARTIAL / major** - Does the whole experience feel like a real production community app for the target users, not merely an implemented workflow harness? Some rows show real domain content, such as Garden Club event details, payment amounts, sender/audience/timestamps, and receipts. However the screenshot set still includes visible persona-picker harness UI and many action/review screens share the same workflow-card renderer and generic lifecycle copy. Required fix: Remove harness surfaces from production evidence and replace generic action/review templates with domain-native screens per workflow.
- **PARTIAL / major** - Is the UI modern, easy to use, easy to navigate, and visually appealing for the target persona? The typography is legible and several screens have clear actions, but the inspected pixels show one-note palettes, very large same-shape cards, repeated chips, and weak component variety across unrelated communities. Required fix: Increase component variety and task-specific hierarchy; use real product layouts such as feeds, inboxes, detail pages, forms, receipts, and consoles instead of the same card stack everywhere.
- **PARTIAL / major** - Is the overall information architecture organized around community content and real jobs-to-be-done instead of workflow lists or validation surfaces? Entry screens often use community content, but action/review screens frequently collapse tasks into generic summaries like Decision queue, Payment summary, Export steps, and Conversation actions. Data portability and platform ad rows read as validation/audit explanations rather than production IA. Required fix: Make every primary workflow land on the product surface named in the product docs: event RSVP, ballot, donation checkout, care request, message thread, ad slot, export console, and so on.
- **NO / major** - Does the visible UI avoid blocking or major overlap, clipping, crowding, default-scaffold, repeated-card, checklist-modal, and thin-content defects? The actual screenshots still show repeated-card fatigue, a local role-selection dialog, generic checklist-like decision queues, thin media/ad evidence, and receiver states with wrong admin/publishing copy. Required fix: Recapture after redesigning the failed surfaces and verify the screenshots no longer show harness dialogs, repeated review shells, thin explanatory cards, or wrong-persona copy.

## Findings
### B25-VISION-P28-MAJ-001 - major
- Community: Multiple communities
- Workflow/persona: multiple action/review workflows / multiple
- Affected rows: 45
- Visible evidence: Visible examples include "Decision queue", "Payment summary", "Export steps", "Conversation actions", and "Confirm the details, editable fields, final progress, and next step before saving."
- Problem: Many action/review screens still read as a shared workflow renderer rather than finished product surfaces. The pixels repeat the same large colored card, outlined token chips, generic progress/review blocks, and catch-all lifecycle language across unrelated communities.
- Required fix: Replace the shared action/review template with workflow-specific product surfaces: event RSVP detail, ballot, payment checkout, document view, review queue, message thread, care request, donation receipt, and export wizard. Remove generic lifecycle copy and make each screen use task-specific hierarchy, fields, alternatives, validation, and receipt/status states.

### B25-VISION-P28-MAJ-002 - major
- Community: Masjid Nur / persona-role-inventory
- Workflow/persona: wf_demo-app-persona-picker and wf_persona-role-inventory-capability-matrix / admin/member
- Affected rows: 4
- Visible evidence: The dialog reads "Choose viewing role" and "Choose how this community should look for each member role" over the Masjid Nur surface.
- Problem: The evidence includes the local persona picker and role inventory as reviewed user-facing screens. This exposes testing/harness mechanics instead of production identity, membership, and permission behavior.
- Required fix: Remove persona-picker and role-inventory harness screens from production UX evidence or redesign them as a true admin/member permissions surface. Production screenshots should show logged-in role state, disabled/hidden actions, and receiver behavior without a "choose viewing role" modal.

### B25-VISION-P28-MAJ-003 - major
- Community: Masjid Nur
- Workflow/persona: wf_multi-persona-workflow-evidence / wf_demo-app-persona-picker / member
- Affected rows: 4
- Visible evidence: Member-facing rows still show "Announcement composer", "Confirm the message body... before publishing", "Preview announcement", and a primary "Receive announcement" button.
- Problem: The member receiver state is not a production inbox/read experience. It reuses admin publishing language and asks the receiver to perform an artificial receive action, so the persona handoff is not visibly credible.
- Required fix: Create separate admin compose/publish screens and member inbox/detail screens. Member screenshots must show received message body, sender, delivery timestamp, read/receipt state, and natural actions such as mark read, reply, save, mute, or open event details, without publish/preview composer copy.

### B25-VISION-P28-MAJ-004 - major
- Community: Data Portability Community
- Workflow/persona: export/import/checksum/rollback workflows / owner
- Affected rows: 11
- Visible evidence: Visible export rows show one repeated "Export steps" checklist and primary actions such as "Generate export" even on rollback, schema listing, import preview, and checksum evidence screens.
- Problem: Data portability workflows are compressed into the same checklist shell. Critical tasks such as rollback, checksum verification, schema exclusion, and import conflict preview do not have distinct, semantically correct action models.
- Required fix: Build a real export/import console with distinct surfaces for schema catalog, redaction preview, checksum verification, import conflict preview, transfer status, and rollback. Use primary actions like Verify checksum, Exclude schema, Resolve conflict, Start rollback, Retry transfer, and Download bundle, with durable audit/result states.

### B25-VISION-P28-MAJ-005 - major
- Community: Camera Club / Member Social Space
- Workflow/persona: critique-submission and platform ad/no-fill workflows / member
- Affected rows: 12
- Visible evidence: The critique screen shows text-only "Image and title" instead of an actual image preview; ad/no-fill rows show explanatory cards such as "Top banner no-fill" and "Sensitive page ad guard" rather than real banner or stream placements.
- Problem: Some primary surfaces still prove concepts with thin explanatory cards. The camera critique lacks the visual artifact being critiqued, and the ad/no-fill evidence is a descriptive audit surface rather than an actual in-stream ad, reserved banner slot, or protected content layout.
- Required fix: Add concrete media and placement proof: critique screens need an image thumbnail/detail, prompt, consent, comments, edit/withdraw, and reviewer/result state. Platform ad screens need actual stream context, sponsor creative/disclosure, no-fill reserved banner space, suppression reason, and dismiss/report/manage affordances in situ.
